## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- MLServer is a Kubernetes component.
- connecting to external services\: kafka, requests.
- 82 Python package dependencies (82 required), categories\: 1 data, 1 grpc, 1 web-server, 14 observability, 2 numerical, 2 serialization, 3 web-framework.
- Python k8s client with 27 API call(s).
- references components\: mlflow (import).
